*Sifat Afroj Moon,Dakotah Maguire,Adam Spannaus,Joe Tuccillo,Maksudul Alam,Sudip K. Seal,John Gounley,Heidi Hanson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HALE框架，将大型语言模型（LLMs）集成到基于智能体的建模（ABM）中，以预测人类决策，从而克服传统ABM依赖静态先验、无法适应实时变化的局限性，并以COVID-19在盐湖县的模拟作为概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ABM依赖静态先验，无法适应实时变化，存在信息缺口，需要一种能够动态预测人类决策的方法。

Method: 提出HALE（混合智能体与语言驱动的流行病建模）框架，将LLMs集成到ABM中，利用LLMs预测ABM模拟中的人类决策行为。

Result: 以COVID-19在犹他州盐湖县的模拟作为概念验证，展示了HALE框架的可行性。

Conclusion: LLMs为ABM提供了一种新的动态预测人类决策的能力，有望增强ABM在政策制定中的实时适应性。

Abstract: Agent-based modeling (ABM) has the capability to model millions of individuals and their interactions, which is useful for policy making. However, ABMs have traditionally relied on static prior, which prevents the models from adapting to real-time changes. Our research provides a novel approach to addressing this information gap. Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to predict human decision-making. Here, we introduce a scalable Hybrid Agent-based and Language-driven Epidemic (HALE) modeling framework that leverages LLMs to predict human decision-making in an ABM simulation. As a proof-of-concept, we use HALE to simulate COVID-19 and its effects in Salt Lake County, UT.

</details>


### [29] [Grounding Spatial Relations in a Compact World Model: Instruction Leakage and a Goal-Free Dynamics Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06925)
*Yufeng Wang,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键问题：在目标条件化世界模型中，看似高精度的关系理解（如“把红色方块放在蓝色左边”）实则是“指令转录”（instruction transcription）而非真正的感知。当指令被隐藏或替换为反事实指令时，模型表现急剧下降，证明存在指令泄漏（instruction leakage）现象。作者在桌面场景和BabyAI基准中验证了这一发现，并提出了诊断方法和修复方案：将目标从动态度中移除，由规划器管理，并监督读取路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标条件化世界模型声称能通过稀疏参考锚点（reference anchors）实现语言目标与空间关系的接地，但作者怀疑这种“理解”可能并非真正的感知，而是模型简单地转录指令中的信息。本文旨在识别并验证这一混淆因素。

Method: 通过三组对照实验：（1）隐藏目标指令，观察模型关系读出准确率从0.90骤降至0.27；（2）使用反事实指令，模型94.5%的概率跟随错误指令而非真实场景；（3）在桌面场景、BabyAI和Language-Table三个设置中进行消融实验，验证指令泄漏的条件。

Result: 指令泄漏的核心条件是：被评分量可以从指令中转录（即指令命名了答案），且与非指令输入的预测能力基本无关。桌面场景和BabyAI基准存在泄漏，而Language-Table的前向动态度模型（指令命名指代对象而非方向）不泄漏。降低动作质量不会增加泄漏（与预测器竞争假说相反）。修复后模型准确率0.88，有无指令表现一致。

Conclusion: 对于任何指令命名了被评分量的目标条件化世界模型，都应检查是否存在指令泄漏。诊断方法是移除指令观察性能变化，修复方法是将目标移出动态度由规划器管理，并监督读取路径。该协议和修复方案具有普适性。

Abstract: Compact world models that condition on a language goal promise to ground relations such as ``put the red block left of the blue block'' using a sparse set of explicit \emph{reference anchors}. We ask when such references actually ground a relation, and identify a trap: a goal-conditioned predictor reaches a striking $0.90$ relation-readout accuracy, yet this is \emph{instruction transcription}, not perception. Withholding the goal collapses it to chance ($0.90\!\to\!0.27$, three seeds) and a counterfactual instruction makes the predicted anchors follow the \emph{false} instruction $94.5\%$ of the time (true scene $2.3\%$; $N{=}256$). Tested across three settings and a within-task ablation, our central claim characterizes the confound: \textbf{instruction leakage occurs when the scored quantity is transcribable from the instruction (when the instruction names the answer) and is essentially independent of how predictive the non-instruction inputs are.} Our tabletop and the external BabyAI benchmark leak, whereas a Language-Table forward-dynamics world model whose instruction names \emph{referents} does not, until the instruction is augmented to name the direction; and degrading the action never increases leakage, the opposite of what predictor-competition predicts. The diagnosis prescribes the fix: keep the goal out of the dynamics (it belongs to the planner's cost) and supervise the \emph{read} path, recovering genuine, instruction-independent grounding ($0.88$, identical with and without the goal). The detection protocol and remedy apply to any goal-conditioned world model whose instruction names the scored quantity.

</details>


### [30] [Large Behavior Model: A Promptable Digital Twin of the Retail Customer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06993)
*Wachiravit Modecrua,Krittin Pachtrachai,Touchapon Kraisingkorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出大型行为模型(LBM)，通过统一的人物-环境框架从大规模零售交易中学习客户决策。该模型采用持续预训练、监督微调和基于可验证奖励的强化学习进行训练，在购买预测、硬负样本区分、购物篮补全、促销响应和跨域优惠券兑换等任务上持续优于通用语言模型。研究表明交易历史中的行为知识可被语言模型有效学习，为客户数字孪生和行为模拟提供可扩展基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么优化预测准确性但缺乏可解释性，要么模拟用户行为但缺乏真实数据支撑，无法同时实现高精度和可解释的客户行为建模。

Method: 提出LBM框架：(1) 人物-环境统一公式化客户状态与产品上下文；(2) 将行为数据口语化后进行持续预训练；(3) 监督微调用于决策生成；(4) 基于可验证奖励的强化学习进行证据校准。

Result: 在多个零售任务上持续优于前沿通用语言模型；消融实验显示持续预训练是行为泛化的主要驱动力，检索在训练和推理时同时应用效果最佳，强化学习能提升对显式行为证据的依赖。

Conclusion: 交易历史中的行为知识可被语言模型有效学习，为构建客户数字孪生和可扩展的行为模拟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Customer behavior modeling underpins recommendation, marketing, and decision support, yet existing approaches either optimize predictive accuracy without explaining decisions or simulate users without grounding them in real behavioral data. We present the Large Behavioral Model (LBM) that learns customer decision making directly from large-scale retail transactions through a unified Person-Environment formulation. Customer state is represented by a behavioral profile derived from historical purchases, while product context is incorporated through retrieval-augmented generation. The model is trained using continued pre-training on verbalized behavioral data, supervised fine-tuning for decision generation, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for evidence-based calibration. We evaluate the proposed framework on purchase prediction, hard-negative discrimination, basket completion, promotion response, and cross-domain voucher redemption. The model consistently outperforms frontier general-purpose language models on in-domain retail tasks while demonstrating strong zero-shot and fine-tuned transfer across retailers and decision domains. Ablation studies show that continued pre-training is the primary driver of behavioral generalization, retrieval is most effective when applied during both training and inference, and reinforcement learning improves reliance on explicit behavioral evidence over generic language-model priors. These results demonstrate that behavioral knowledge encoded in transaction histories can be effectively learned by language models, providing a scalable foundation for customer digital twins and behavior simulation.

</details>


### [31] [Measuring Intelligence Beyond Human Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07040)
*Jerry Han,Rafael Moschopoulos,Ella Colby,Vishrut Goyal,Andrew Tu,Kia Ghods,Mark Braverman,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对抗性心理测量的相对评估范式，通过让模型生成公开挑战来区分不同系统，从而在超越人类能力范围时仍能持续衡量智能水平，并设计了减少隐私攻击、支持无裁判裁决的实用协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类标注的基准测试容易饱和，当系统能力超越人类时，评估者难以知道哪些任务既困难又可验证，这种困难是绝对尺度评估固有的。

Method: 提出基于相对测量的新范式：模型生成公开挑战来区分其他系统，聚合这些结果形成对抗性心理测量评级系统；设计实用协议以减少私有信息攻击的动机，支持无裁判裁决，并自然随系统能力扩展。

Result: 该框架在可验证和开放性、不可验证领域均有实例化，展示了模型生成的评估可以在超越人类前沿时继续衡量系统能力。

Conclusion: 相对测量范式能够解决绝对尺度评估在超越人类能力时的固有困难，为持续衡量超人类智能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: How can we measure intelligence beyond human capability?
  Human-authored benchmarks saturate, and above human capability, examiners may not know which tasks are both hard and verifiable. We argue that this difficulty is inherent to absolute-scale evaluation and propose a new paradigm based on relative measurement in which models generate public challenges that separate other systems. Aggregating these outcomes yields an adversarial psychometric rating system that can scale with the systems being measured. We describe practical protocols that reduce incentives for private-information attacks, support judge-free adjudication, and naturally scale with agent capabilities. We instantiate the framework across verifiable and open-ended, non-verifiable domains, illustrating how model-generated evaluation can continue to measure systems beyond the human frontier.

</details>


### [32] [Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07097)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang,Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证多智能体LLM系统的安全评估中，聚合的"管道效应"混淆了三种机制（操作性重构、规划者拒绝/转化、执行者代理），并提出五条件对比设计。实验表明，管道安全非稳定架构属性，原始模型排名无法预测实际部署行为，安全评估应分别报告各机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体LLM系统安全评估将"直接提示"与"规划-执行流水线"的差异简单归为"管道效应"，但这种聚合指标难以解释，因为它混淆了三种不同的机制：有害意图被重构为合理操作、规划者拒绝或转化请求、执行者在代理提示下行动。作者旨在分离这些因素以更准确地评估架构安全。

Method: 引入五条件受控对比设计，在30个合成有害场景和来自四个智能体安全基准的探索性外部验证集上评估，使用LLM评判的合规性作为指标。

Result: 1) 聚合管道安全非稳定架构属性；2) 操作性重构是最具可迁移性的风险信号，GPT/Gemini/DeepSeek均受影响，Claude较抵抗；3) 规划者通过拒绝可抵消风险，但若产生可执行步骤，执行者合规性可能高于直接操作基线；4) 批准框架的代理对提示设计、模型配对和场景来源敏感；5) 原始模型排名无法预测部署行为，如Gemini在原始提示中最安全，但搭配Claude规划者后合规率从8.9%升至38.9%。

Conclusion: 多智能体安全评估应分别报告重构效应、规划者行为、代理框架和模型配对，而非将失败简单归因于架构本身。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single "pipeline effect." We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance.
  Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance.
  Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself.

</details>


### [33] [Does AI Understand Imaging? A Systematic Benchmark of Agentic AI for Computational Imaging Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07189)
*Ethan Chung,Chuanjun Zheng,Jasper Tan,Jingxi Li,Haopeng Zhang,Huaijin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ImagingBench基准，包含20个计算成像任务，评估视觉语言模型在物理驱动成像问题上的表现。结果表明，现有VLM和智能体方法在计算成像任务上远逊于专用方法，存在从语义视觉到物理成像能力之间的显著鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM和智能体AI在语义视觉任务上表现优异，但在需要物理理解和逆问题求解的计算成像领域是否有效尚不清楚，因此需要统一基准来系统评估这一差距。

Method: 构建包含20个任务的ImagingBench基准，覆盖射线/波动光学、图像信号处理、逆重建、计算感知和标定五大类。设置三种评估模式：Expert（固定专家引导逆重建）、Planner（规划器引导逆重建）和Forward（前向仿真一致性检查）。评测Gemini、GPT、Qwen等主流多模态系统并与任务特定非智能体基线对比。

Result: 智能体模型在所有任务上始终弱于专用方法，尤其在无透镜成像、事件重建、飞行时间成像和全息等计算感知问题上差距明显。Planner引导相比Expert基线提升有限且不稳定，模型虽能生成视觉上合理的输出，但基于参考的保真度很差。

Conclusion: VLM和智能体AI在语义视觉能力与物理驱动成像性能之间存在显著鸿沟，ImagingBench为衡量这一差距和追踪计算成像智能体AI的进展提供了统一的测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) and agentic AI have shown strong performance on semantic visual tasks, but it remains unclear whether they can handle the physics and inverse problems that underlie computational imaging. We present ImagingBench, a benchmark of 20 computational imaging tasks spanning five categories: ray and wave optics, image signal processing, inverse reconstruction, computational sensing, and calibration. ImagingBench evaluates three complementary settings: Expert, fixed expert-guided inverse reconstruction; Planner, planner-guided inverse reconstruction; and Forward, forward-system simulation for consistency checking. We benchmark leading proprietary and open-source image-centric multimodal systems, including Gemini, GPT, and Qwen, and compare them with representative task-specific non-agentic baselines. Across tasks, agentic models remain consistently weaker than specialized methods, especially on computational sensing problems such as lensless imaging, event-based reconstruction, time-of-flight imaging, and holography. Planner guidance provides only modest and inconsistent gains over the fixed-prompt Expert baseline. Although the models often generate visually plausible outputs, their reference-based fidelity remains poor, revealing a substantial gap between semantic visual competence and physically grounded imaging performance. ImagingBench provides a unified testbed for measuring this gap and tracking progress in agentic AI for computational imaging.

</details>


### [34] [From Atomic Actions to Standard Operating Procedures: Iterative Tool Optimization for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07321)
*Haipeng Ding,Yuexiang Xie,Zhewei Wei,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoSOP框架，让LLM代理将原子操作合成为可复用的标准操作程序（SOPs），通过构建、合并、评估和剪枝的生命周期实现工具集的自我进化，显著提升任务成功率并减少交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理框架依赖静态的细粒度原子动作工具集，导致代理在每个重复工作流中都需要重新实现底层逻辑，增加了推理开销和失败率。

Method: 提出EvoSOP框架，使代理能从执行轨迹中提取SOPs，并通过构建、合并、评估和剪枝的系统化生命周期迭代优化工具集。

Result: 实验表明EvoSOP显著提升了任务成功率，同时大幅减少了交互轮次。

Conclusion: 迭代工具优化能够催生可靠且高效的工具使用模式，为自进化代理的开发提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: Tool utilization enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to interact with the real world and resolve complex tasks. However, existing agent frameworks predominantly rely on static toolsets composed of granular atomic actions (e.g., basic file I/O or single-turn search), which forces agents to reinvent low-level logic for every recurring workflow, leading to increased reasoning overhead and failure rates. In this study, we propose that agents can achieve self-evolution by synthesizing these atomic actions into reusable Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs), which function as callable higher-order tools that encapsulate multi-step logic. We further introduce EvoSOP, a framework that empowers agents to extract SOPs from execution trajectories and iteratively optimize the toolset through a systematic lifecycle of construction, merging, evaluation, and pruning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvoSOP significantly boosts task success rates while substantially reducing the number of interaction rounds compared to baselines. Our analysis also reveals that iterative tool optimization fosters reliable and efficient tool-use patterns, providing a scalable pathway for the development of self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [35] [MIRA-Math: A Benchmark for Minimal Information Requesting and Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07391)
*Charbel Al Bateh,Samer Saab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRA-Math是一个数学推理基准，专注于评估模型在缺失一个关键原子事实时主动请求缺失信息并整合求解的能力，包含2,310个实例和22种数学类型，实验表明请求成功与答案正确性是可分离的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准通常提供解题所需的全部事实，而交互式基准则混杂了推理、工具使用、检索和长程对话，缺乏对"在缺失一条必要原子事实时主动请求信息"这一诊断能力的精准评估。

Method: 构建MIRA-Math基准：每个问题在完整潜在状态下有唯一答案，但求解者视角恰好缺失一个必要的原子事实。求解者需在严格预算下用自然语言请求缺失信息，然后整合返回的事实得到精确答案。一个固定的受限LLM响应器仅能提供数据集给定的原子事实——当请求匹配时引用该事实，否则拒绝。实例生成、提示类型说明、验证和答案校验都是确定性的，请求指标在固定LLM中介响应通道下测量。包含2,310个实例，涵盖代数、概率、线性系统、离散结构、信号处理、马尔可夫链、电路、插值和数值边值问题等22种数学类型。

Result: 在前沿和小型模型上的实验表明，请求成功与最终答案准确率是可分离的：模型可能请求了正确的事实但后续计算失败，也可能在获得标准提示之前就失败。

Conclusion: 发布完整的生成器、验证器、提示、运行元数据和数据集文档，支持数学推理中最小信息请求的可重复评估。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning benchmarks typically provide all facts needed to solve each problem, while interactive benchmarks often mix reasoning with tools, retrieval, and long-horizon dialogue. We introduce MIRA-Math, a benchmark for a narrower diagnostic capability: solving mathematical problems whose full latent state has a unique answer, but whose solver-facing view is missing exactly one necessary atomic fact. The solver must request the missing information in natural language under a strict budget and then integrate the returned fact into an exact final answer. A fixed constrained LLM responder sees only the dataset-provided atomic fact and must either offer the quoted fact when the request matches it, or decline otherwise. Thus, instance generation, typed hint specifications, validation, and final-answer verification are deterministic, while request metrics are measured under a fixed LLM-mediated responder channel. MIRA-Math contains 2{,}310 generated instances from 22 typed mathematical families spanning algebra, probability, linear systems, discrete structures, signal processing, Markov chains, circuits, interpolation, and numerical boundary-value problems. Experiments across frontier and small models show that request success and final-answer accuracy are separable: models may ask for the right fact yet fail the downstream computation, or fail before obtaining the canonical hint. We release generators, verifiers, prompts, run metadata, and dataset documentation to support reproducible evaluation of minimal information requesting in mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [36] [Reason Less, Verify More: Deterministic Gates Recover a Silent Policy-Violation Failure Mode in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07405)
*Vikas Reddy,Sumanth Reddy Challaram,Abhishek Basu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了LLM智能体在策略许可环境下会执行违反域策略的工具调用导致静默错误状态的问题。提出了一种确定性只读预执行门控机制，在工具执行前检查调用和状态。在τ²-bench航空领域，四门控套件将gpt-4o-mini成功率从29.6%提升至42.0%（+12.4pp），并在15个独立种子上复现（+12.3pp）。效果集中在门控触发的26/50任务上（+19.2pp），非触发任务无显著变化。在更先进的gpt-5.2上同样有效（61.2%→71.6%，+10.4pp）。贡献在于提供了有边界的评估和可靠性结果：确定性门控不能保证任务成功，但能确定性地防止已知类别的静默策略违规写入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在策略许可环境中会执行违反域策略的工具调用，导致静默错误状态——工具和智能体自身报告均无法暴露这些错误。现有系统缺乏对这种静默故障模式的系统研究，需要轻量级干预来防止此类策略违规写入。

Method: 提出确定性只读预执行门控机制（pre-execution gates），在工具执行前检查提议的工具调用和当前状态，只有通过检查才允许写操作。在τ²-bench航空域构建四门控套件，并在gpt-4o-mini和gpt-5.2上评估。使用配对任务级bootstrap进行显著性检验，并通过15个独立种子验证可重复性。设置两个负对照实验（自强制零售域和BFCL）来界定机制边界。

Result: 在τ²-bench航空域，78%的失败是静默错误状态（无工具错误）。四门控套件在gpt-4o-mini上成功率从29.6%提升至42.0%（+12.4pp；P=0.0012），15个独立种子复现+12.3pp（P=0.0008）。效果集中在门控触发的26个任务上（+19.2pp），非触发任务无显著变化。在gpt-5.2上成功率从61.2%提升至71.6%（+10.4pp；P=0.020；n=5）。负对照实验验证了机制边界。

Conclusion: 确定性门控不能保证任务成功，但能确定性地防止已知类别的静默策略违规写入，这是一种有边界的可靠性结果。该故障模式在更先进的模型（gpt-5.2）中仍然存在，表明这是一个持续性的安全问题。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents can violate the very policies they are deployed to enforce while appearing to complete the task successfully. In policy-permissive environments, a tool may execute any well-formed call even when the corresponding state transition is forbidden by domain policy. The result is a silent wrong state (a booking cancelled, a passenger count changed, a claim acted on without verification) that neither the tool nor the agent's self-report exposes.
  We study this failure mode in the $τ^2$-bench airline domain. On a budget agent, 78% of observed failures are silent wrong-state failures with no tool error, and the aggregate failure rate is reproducible across disjoint seeds, not sampling noise. We then evaluate a lightweight intervention: deterministic, read-only pre-execution gates that inspect the proposed call and current state before allowing a write. A four-gate suite raises full-benchmark success from 29.6% to 42.0% on gpt-4o-mini (+12.4pp; paired task-level bootstrap P=0.0012), and the lift reproduces on a disjoint 15-seed set (+12.3pp; P=0.0008).
  The effect is concentrated where the gates fire: on the 26/50 firing tasks, success rises by +19.2pp, while movement on the 24 non-firing tasks does not exclude zero. Two negative controls (a self-enforcing retail domain and BFCL) bound the mechanism: gates help when tools are policy-permissive and add little where tools already self-enforce. As suggestive evidence, not a central claim, the same failure mode persists at the frontier: gpt-5.2 at default reasoning still attempts policy-violating writes, and the same suite improves success from 61.2% to 71.6% (+10.4pp; P=0.020; n=5, no replication). The contribution is a bounded evaluation and reliability result: deterministic gates do not guarantee task success, but they can deterministically prevent a known class of silent policy-violating writes at the action boundary.

</details>


### [37] [InductWave: Inductive Multi-Hop Logical Query Answering on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07422)
*Mayank Kharbanda,Michael Cochez,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Raghava Mutharaju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InductWave，一种基于小波的inductive嵌入方法，用于大规模知识图谱上的逻辑多跳查询回答。该方法在仅使用基模型一半消息传递层数的情况下达到同等性能，且75%层数时超越所有基线模型，能处理训练中未见过的实体，并在大规模图（如Wiki-KG）上有效运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG逻辑查询方法大多基于transductive推理，无法处理训练中未见的实体；现实世界中大规模KG存在资源稀缺问题，无法对所有节点进行训练，因此需要一种inductive的、资源高效的逻辑查询回答方法。

Method: 提出InductWave，一种基于小波变换的inductive嵌入方法。训练图包含比测试图更少的节点，模型通过小波变换实现归纳推理，使用更少的消息传递层数（基模型的一半）完成逻辑多跳查询回答。

Result: 在FB15k-237数据集上进行了不同训练/测试图比例的大量实验。InductWave在75%层数时即可超越所有基线模型，使用50%层数时性能与基线持平。能够在Wiki-KG等大规模图上进行评估。

Conclusion: InductWave通过小波变换实现了高效的inductive逻辑查询回答，显著降低了资源需求（更少的消息传递层数），同时保持了优越的性能，可扩展至大规模知识图谱。

Abstract: Logical Multi-Hop Query Answering over Knowledge Graphs (KGs) can be formulated as querying, with an implicit completeness assumption. Current works mainly focus on Existential First Order Logic (EFO) queries. These EFO queries contain conjunction, disjunction, and negation operators. Most existing works employ transductive reasoning, meaning they are not capable of reasoning over entities unseen during training. In the real world, there is a resource scarcity, and we cannot train a model with all the nodes of a large KG. Hence, we propose InductWave, a wavelet-based inductive embedding method for logical query answering on large KGs. Here, the training graph consists of fewer nodes than the test graph. Our model performs on par with the baseline models while having half the number of message-passing layers. It outperforms all of them in most cases, with 75% of the layers. These fewer resource requirements enable us to evaluate InductWave on massive graphs, such as Wiki-KG. We test our model using extensive experiments across varying train-test graph proportions of the FB15k-(237) dataset, comparing it with the state-of-the-art models. The code and datasets for the model are available at https://github.com/kracr/inductwave/.

</details>


### [38] [SpaCellAgent: A Self-Evolving LLM-Based Multi-Agent Framework for Trajectory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07467)
*Songhan Wang,Haoang Chi,He Li,Zhiheng Zhang,Jiayan Yuan,Cheems Wang,Hao Peng,Xinwang Liu,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpaCellAgent是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，可自动完成从自然语言指令到时空转录组学轨迹推断的端到端分析流程，相比现有方法效率提升超40%，同时保持专家级别的分析质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹推断方法需要大量人工干预且需熟练使用多种异构工具，缺乏自动化端到端解决方案，阻碍了高效的空间与单细胞转录组轨迹分析。

Method: 采用多智能体架构进行策略性工作流规划，动态工具编排引擎实现自适应算法选择，以及通过反馈迭代优化性能的自我进化模块，将自然语言指令转化为优化的分析工作流。

Result: 在6个异质数据集（涵盖复杂时间发育轨迹、多种测序平台、空间分辨组织架构）上，分析效率提升超40%，同时保持与专家一致的分析性能。

Conclusion: SpaCellAgent通过全自动流水线将自然语言规范转化为优化分析工作流，民主化了高级时空建模方法，建立了可扩展的智能体驱动计算生物学范式。

Abstract: Spatial and Single-cell transcriptomics are transformative in deciphering cellular dynamics. As the fundamental paradigm for reconstructing cell developmental paths, trajectory inference (TI) is critical. However, existing methods require extensive manual intervention and proficiency in heterogeneous tools, posing a significant barrier to efficient TI analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose SpaCellAgent, an autonomous large language model (LLM) multi-agent framework that automates end-to-end spatiotemporal analysis and narrative generation. SpaCellAgent utilizes a multi-agent architecture for strategic workflow planning, a dynamic tool-orchestration engine for adaptive algorithm selection, and a self-evolution module that iteratively refines performance through feedback. We evaluate SpaCellAgent on six heterogeneous datasets encompassing complex temporal developmental trajectories, diverse sequencing platforms, and spatially-resolved tissue architectures. SpaCellAgent consistently demonstrates over 40\% improvement in analytical efficiency while maintaining expert-aligned performance. By converting natural language specifications into optimized analytical workflows and fully automating the pipeline, SpaCellAgent democratizes advanced spatiotemporal modeling and establishes a scalable, agent-driven paradigm for computational biology. The code and materials are available at https://github.com/LittleXH-shw/SpaCellAgent.

</details>


### [39] [RL Post-Training Builds Compositional Reasoning Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07646)
*Azwar Abdulsalam,Nishil Patel,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过可观测的重写语法环境，研究RL后训练是否仅放大基座模型已有的原始技能，还是能组合出新的高阶策略。实验表明，RL能通过阶段性组合机制将原始能力重组为可靠的高阶策略（包括序列组合和并行组合），而拒绝微调（RFT）则产生大量无效捷径。关键在于选择性而非探索量，且组合策略的出现依赖于预训练是否将原始能力组织为可压缩的约简过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RL后训练是仅仅放大基座模型已有的原始技能，还是能将原始技能组合成新的高阶策略，以理解RL在推理能力涌现中的作用机制。

Method: 在可观测的重写语法环境中，Transformer在原始符号重写链上预训练，然后在基于Trace的推理任务上（仅提供二元最终答案奖励）进行RL后训练。通过对比RL与拒绝微调（RFT），并分析回溯轨迹、预训练消融实验来揭示机制。

Result: RL能解决基座模型即使在大采样预算下也难以解决的难题，而RFT早期有提升但随后停滞。RL通过分阶段组合机制：先强化原始约简，再发现有效组合过程（序列组合和并行组合），并将这些组合过程巩固为稳定技能库。关键差异在于选择性而非探索量——RFT产生大量无效捷径，RL则将探索集中在有效可重用结构上。组合策略的出现取决于预训练是否将原始能力组织为可压缩的约简过程。

Conclusion: RL后训练不仅仅是放大原始技能，而是能将原始程序性成分重组为可靠的高阶策略。预训练提供弱程序性成分，RL将其构建为可靠的高阶策略。组合策略的涌现需要预训练以特定方式组织原始能力，而非仅靠原始技能暴露。

Abstract: Does RL post-training merely amplify primitive skills already latent in a base model, or can it compose primitive skills into new higher-level strategies? We study this question in a fully observable rewrite-grammar environment where the pretraining distribution is known and every generated rewrite can be audited. A Transformer is pretrained on primitive symbol-rewrite chains and post-trained on a Trace-based reasoning task with only a binary final-answer reward. RL solves held-out problems that remain rarely solved by the pretrained model even under much larger sampling budgets, while rejection fine-tuning improves early but plateaus. Trace analysis shows that RL reorganizes primitive competence through a phased compositional mechanism: it first strengthens primitive reductions, then discovers valid composed procedures. These include sequential compositions, which collapse ordered chains of primitive contractions, and parallel compositions, which combine independent primitive contractions in a single step. The composed procedures are not isolated samples; they are reused and consolidated into a stable repertoire. Comparing RL with rejection fine-tuning shows that the key difference is not exploration volume but selectivity: RFT produces many shortcut-like rewrites, much of them invalid, whereas RL concentrates exploration into valid reusable structure. Pretraining ablations show that the emergence of compositional strategies is gated not by primitive exposure alone, but by whether pretraining organizes primitive competence into reduction procedures that RL can later compress. The base model provides weak procedural ingredients; RL builds them into reliable higher-level strategies.

</details>


### [40] [SkillCenter: A Large-Scale Source-Grounded Skill Library for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07676)
*Tianming Sha,Yue Zhao,Lichao Sun,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillCenter是当前最大的开源智能体技能库，包含216,938个结构化技能，覆盖24个领域。通过SkillGate质量过滤管道，整合了114,565个源自同行评审期刊、ArXiv等学术来源的技能和102,373个社区技能，并提供离线可搜索的SQLite FTS5包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在执行复杂任务时缺乏 grounded 的操作知识，导致输出在正确性、安全性和可维护性方面存在不足，亟需一个大规模、来源可追溯的高质量技能库。

Method: 提出端到端框架，包括多源采集（学术与社区）、基于LLM的质量门控（SkillGate）、模板驱动生成、迭代源接地（确保每个声明可追溯到源引文）以及质量控制发布。技能以离线可搜索的SQLite FTS5包形式分发。

Result: 构建了包含216,938个结构化技能的技能库，其中114,565个来源于24,000+技术资料（经SkillGate过滤），102,373个来自GitHub和ClawHub社区。所有技能均通过源接地保证可追溯性。

Conclusion: SkillCenter通过大规模、来源可追溯的技能库，为AI智能体提供了可靠的操作知识基础，兼顾了技能的正确性、安全性和可维护性。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents can execute complex tasks with limited human review, yet they often lack the grounded operational knowledge to make their outputs not just executable but correct, secure, and maintainable. We introduce SkillCenter, to our knowledge the largest open skill library for agents by total count: 216,938 structured skills across 24 domain bundles. A SkillGate-filtered pipeline contributes 114,565 source-grounded skills from peer-reviewed journals, ArXiv, and over 24,000 technical sources, integrated with 102,373 community skills from GitHub and the ClawHub marketplace. We present the end-to-end framework that builds the pipeline subset: multi-source acquisition, an LLM-based quality gate (SkillGate), template-driven generation, iterative source-grounding, and quality-controlled publishing. Source grounding is a traceability guarantee: each retained claim maps to an exact quotation in its source. All skills ship as offline-searchable SQLite FTS5 bundles.

</details>

### [41] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Reliability Based Bi-Objective Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06610)
*Sounaq Das,Tanmay Sen,Raghu Nandan Sengupta,Aditya Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出深度强化学习框架MORP-DRL，用于多目标可靠性组合优化，联合优化期望收益与下行风险，在十只全球股指数据上验证了其在风险-收益表现、下行风险控制和可扩展性方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可靠性的组合优化方法多采用静态优化框架，难以处理序贯决策、尾部风险以及交易成本等市场摩擦问题。

Method: 构建深度强化学习框架，采用方差、CVaR和EVaR三种互补风险度量；使用GARCH(1,1)、极值理论和t-copula建模资产收益；通过准蒙特卡洛模拟生成场景；基于PPO算法在交易成本和投资组合边界约束下进行优化，并与NSGA-II进行对比。

Result: 在COVID前后及期间三个市场阶段、十只全球股指上的实验表明，MORP-DRL取得了有竞争力的风险-收益表现，在市场压力时期降低了下行风险，并具备高维组合可扩展性。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习能够有效解决多目标可靠性组合优化问题，兼顾收益、多种尾部风险度量与市场摩擦约束，优于传统静态优化方法。

Abstract: Portfolio optimization under uncertainty is inherently a multi-objective decision problem involving complex interactions among return, risk, market dynamics, and practical investment constraints. Existing reliability based portfolio optimization approaches primarily rely on static optimization frameworks and often fail to capture sequential decision making, tail risk, and market frictions such as transaction costs. To address these limitations, we propose a deep reinforcement learning framework for multi-objective reliability based portfolio optimization (MORP-DRL). The proposed framework jointly optimizes expected return and downside risk using three complementary risk measures: variance, Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR), and Entropic Value-at-Risk (EVaR). To model uncertainty and heavy-tailed market behavior, asset returns are represented using GARCH(1,1), Extreme Value Theory, and a t-copula dependence structure, while realistic scenarios are generated through quasi-Monte Carlo simulation. A Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) based strategy is developed under practical constraints including transaction costs and portfolio bounds, and is benchmarked against NSGA-II. Experiments on ten global equity indices across pre-COVID, COVID, and post-COVID market regimes demonstrate that MORP-DRL achieves competitive risk-return performance, reduced downside risk during periods of market stress, and scalability to high-dimensional portfolio settings.

</details>


### [42] [STAGformer: A Spatio-temporal Agent Graph Transformer for Micro Mobility Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06614)
*Ye Zihao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAGformer提出了一种基于智能体注意力机制（Agent Attention）的时空变换器，通过引入少量可学习的空间和时间智能体令牌，将标准自注意力的二次复杂度降至线性O(NT)，在共享单车流量预测任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车系统中准确的车站级需求预测对高效运营至关重要，但由于复杂的时空依赖性和大规模城市网络，现有方法（尤其是标准Transformer）存在二次计算复杂度的瓶颈，难以高效建模全局依赖关系。

Method: 提出STAGformer模型，核心创新为两阶段智能体注意力机制：第一阶段用少量可学习的空间/时间智能体令牌聚合全局信息，第二阶段将信息广播回各车站和时间步。模型包含四个模块：时空编码器（融合动态节点特征与天气、时间、兴趣点等外部因素）、图传播模块（空间邻居聚合）、时间卷积模块（局部模式提取）和智能体注意力模块（全局依赖建模）。

Result: 在纽约Citi-Bike和芝加哥Divvy-Bike两个真实数据集上，STAGformer在多个预测时域上均一致超越所有基线方法，RMSE和MAE均有显著改进。消融实验验证了各模块的有效性，其中智能体注意力机制对全局时空依赖建模最为关键。

Conclusion: STAGformer通过智能体注意力机制实现了高效的线性复杂度全局建模，在不牺牲性能的前提下解决了标准Transformer在时空预测任务中的计算效率问题，为大规模城市级共享单车需求预测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate station-level demand forecasting is essential for the efficient operation of bike-sharing systems, yet it remains challenging due to complex spatio-temporal dependencies and the large scale of urban networks. This paper presents STAGformer, a Spatio-Temporal Agent Graph Transformer that achieves efficient global modeling with linear computational complexity. The model introduces a two-step agent attention mechanism, where a small set of learnable spatial and temporal agent tokens first aggregate global information and then broadcast it back to individual stations and time steps, effectively capturing long-range interactions while reducing the quadratic cost of standard self-attention to O(NT). STAGformer integrates four core modules: a spatio-temporal encoder that fuses dynamic node features with external contextual factors (weather, time, points of interest), a graph propagation module for spatial neighbor aggregation, a temporal convolution module for local pattern extraction, and the agent attention module for global dependency modeling. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets -- NYC Citi-Bike and Chicago Divvy-Bike -- demonstrate that STAGformer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple prediction horizons, achieving significant improvements in both RMSE and MAE. Ablation studies validate the contribution of each component, with the agent attention mechanism proving critical for modeling global spatio-temporal dependencies.

</details>


### [43] [WHERE to Generate Matters: Budget-Aware Synthetic Augmentation for Label Skewed Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06616)
*Sangwoo Lee,Sunghwan Park,Jaewoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedEAS通过熵自适应预算分配策略，在联邦学习中仅用5.9%的生成预算即可恢复全类别平衡的精度提升，同时超越均匀分配方法18.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中标签分布不均导致客户端漂移和全局精度下降，现有合成数据增强方法虽能缓解但全类别平衡计算成本过高。

Method: 提出FedEAS策略，为每个客户端基于其本地标签分布计算熵自适应的每类生成预算，该预算联合决定生成样本的数量和分发目标，总预算由客户端预算动态决定而非预设。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，FedEAS以94.1%的预算缩减恢复全类别平衡的大部分精度提升，在相同总预算下超越均匀分配方法18.82%。

Conclusion: 熵自适应预算分配能高效缓解标签不平衡问题，实现大幅降低计算成本的同时保持模型精度。

Abstract: Label skew in federated learning (FL) causes client drift and degrades global accuracy. Synthetic data augmentation can reduce this imbalance; however, full class balancing requires substantial computation cost. We propose FedEAS, a policy that assigns each client an entropy-adaptive per-class generation budget computed from its local label distribution. The budget jointly decides \emph{how much} each client generates and \emph{WHERE} the samples go. Accordingly, the total generation budget follows from the per-client budgets rather than being fixed in advance. FedEAS recovers most of the accuracy gain of full class balancing while reducing the generation budget by 94.1\%. At the same total generation budget, it outperforms Uniform allocation by up to 18.82\% across CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100.

</details>


### [44] [LLM-Guided Task-Semantic Field Factorization for Industrial Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06623)
*Youcheng Zong,Runda Jia,Mingxuan Ren,Dakuo He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSF是一个轻量级LLM引导框架，通过离线构建任务语义场，将过程文档中的变量语义信息注入时间序列预测，在多个工业软测量任务上平均降低MAE 6.4%，仅增加约1.8-3k参数和极小的在线推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程中标注数据稀缺、操作工况频繁变化，现有时间序列模型将输入视为匿名数值列，忽略了变量文档中蕴含的语义逻辑关系（如变量名、单位、物理含义、过程角色），导致模型难以适应不同预测目标和工况变化。

Method: TSF框架：离线阶段利用LLM从任务协议和变量文档构建任务语义场；在线训练和推理阶段，当前数值窗口激活对应变量语义，使语义信息参与每个预测步骤，支持不同预测目标和操作偏移的适应。LLM仅用于离线语义构建，在线沿用传统时间序列骨干。

Result: 在多个复杂工业预测和软感知任务上，TSF平均降低MAE 6.4%，最大降低25.5%。仅增加约1.8-3k参数，在线推理额外开销小于0.008 ms/step。

Conclusion: TSF能够将现有过程文档转化为可衡量的预测增益，跨骨干网络和语义生成器均有效，且保持轻量化，适合工业部署。

Abstract: Process industries rely on time-series forecasting and soft sensing to estimate quality variables that are hard to measure online. Labeled data are scarce, operating regimes change frequently, and retraining models or rebuilding alignment pipelines for each scenario is costly. Such settings often provide variable tables and process documents that record variable names, units, physical meanings, and process roles. However, standard time-series backbones usually treat inputs as anonymous numerical columns. Existing text-enhanced methods also rarely make the semantic-logical relations between input variables and the prediction target available to the model within each numerical window. To address this problem, this article proposes Task-Semantic Field Factorization (TSF), a large language model (LLM)-guided framework. TSF builds a task-semantic field from task protocols and variable documents before training and uses the LLM only for offline semantic construction. Online training and inference remain with conventional time-series backbones. During training and inference, the current numerical window activates variable semantics, so semantic information participates in each prediction and supports adaptation to different prediction targets and operating shifts. On multiple complex industrial forecasting and soft-sensing tasks, TSF reduces MAE by 6.4\% on average in improved settings, with the largest reduction reaching 25.5\%. It adds only about 1.8--3.0k parameters, with less than 0.008 ms/step of additional online inference overhead. These results show that TSF turns existing process documents into measurable forecasting gains across backbones and semantic generators while remaining lightweight for deployment.

</details>


### [45] [STST-JEPA: Shallow-Target Spatio-Temporal Joint Embedding Prediction Architecture For EEG Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06629)
*Roy Segal,Yoni Svechinsky,Tomer Fekete*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STST-JEPA，一种自监督EEG Transformer模型，在47,703个会话上预训练（年龄5-81岁），结合潜空间预测与信号重建目标。在年龄回归任务上达到3.06年MAE（r=0.924），在NeuralBench榜单上取得性别分类、年龄预测和精神病理学回归三项第一，且年龄预测残差与认知效率负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电图（EEG）脑年龄是一种有前景的生物标志物，但现有EEG模型面临跨站点导联异构、标注样本少、受试者非平稳性等挑战，且缺乏在完整年龄段（儿童到老年人）上具有竞争力的EEG基础模型。

Method: 基于自监督Transformer（STST-JEPA），采用潜空间预测目标（对掩码token表示进行预测，以tokenizer的EMA作为目标）和辅助信号重建项。在30秒多通道窗口上应用时空块掩码，预训练数据来自brain.space和HBN数据集共47,703个会话。

Result: 冻结嵌入+轻量注意力探针在3,367个会话上年龄回归MAE=3.06年（r=0.924），对比预测均值基线约10年MAE。微调后模型在NeuralBench榜单上取得性别分类（平衡准确率0.911）、年龄预测（r=0.749）和精神病理学回归（r=0.215）三项第一。年龄预测残差与认知效率呈负相关。

Conclusion: STST-JEPA证明了在完整年龄段上基于EEG的脑年龄预测的可行性，且其残差具有认知相关性，为EEG基础模型在临床生物标志物中的应用提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Brain age -- the age inferred from a physiological recording -- is an emerging biomarker whose deviation from chronological age tracks neurological and psychiatric burden, and EEG is an attractive substrate for it because it is cheap, portable, and temporally rich. Yet EEG brain-age models must contend with cross-site montage heterogeneity, small labelled cohorts, and dominant subject-level non-stationarity, and few EEG foundation models have been shown to deliver competitive age regression across the full pediatric-to-older-adult range in which such a biomarker would actually be deployed. We introduce STST-JEPA, a self-supervised transformer for resting-state and task EEG, pretrained on 47,703 sessions spanning ages 5-81 from the brain.space and Healthy Brain Network (HBN) corpora. The model combines a latent-prediction objective - predicting masked-token representations against an EMA-of-tokenizer target - with an auxiliary signal-reconstruction term, applied to 30-second multi-channel windows under spatiotemporal block masks. A lightweight attentive probe trained on frozen pretrained embeddings achieves a best held-out-validation mean absolute error of 3.06 years (r = 0.924) for age regression on 3,367 sessions, against a predict-the-mean baseline of approximately 10 years MAE. With light task-specific fine-tuning of the model's final layers, the same pretrained encoder achieves rank-1 placements - with the model's native 30-second windows - on the public NeuralBench x brain.space EEG leaderboard for sex classification (balanced accuracy 0.911), age prediction (r = 0.749), and psychopathology composite regression (r = 0.215). We further show that the model's age-prediction residual is negatively correlated with cognitive efficiency over several tasks we examined.

</details>


### [46] [Does Demand Response Increase Vulnerability to Cyber Attacks by Adversarial Data Modifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06632)
*Clemens Kortmann,Eike Cramer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗性攻击如何通过操纵电价预测影响工业需求响应的调度决策，发现攻击会侵蚀需求响应利润，但在扰动有限时仍可保留约90%的财务优势；攻击影响不仅取决于扰动幅度，更取决于扰动方向，因此建议将调度优化模型的敏感性纳入攻击设计以进行更严格的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献主要关注对抗性攻击对电力系统预测和电网状态估计的影响，而很少分析其对需求侧的潜在影响。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究操纵电价预测如何影响工业需求响应中的决策制定。

Method: 设计针对电价预测模型的对抗性攻击，通过扭曲的价格预测求解高能耗生产过程的调度优化问题；使用广义过程模型，考察不同工艺灵活性水平的生产调度问题对对抗性攻击的脆弱性。

Result: 对抗性攻击会侵蚀需求响应产生的利润；但当扰动幅度有限（难以被人类用户察觉）时，需求响应仍能保留约90%相比于稳态运行的经济优势；攻击影响主要取决于对抗扰动的方向而非幅度。

Conclusion: 攻击分析应将调度优化模型的敏感性显式纳入攻击设计，以实现对对抗性攻击下决策制定的更严格评估。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks are crafted data manipulations that aim to deteriorate the outcomes of prediction or decision-making algorithms. In the energy systems literature, adversarial attacks have been studied with a focus on problems regarding the electricity grid. Such problems include forecasting and grid state estimation, where adversarial attacks are also known as false data injection attacks. Only few studies have analyzed the potential impact that adversarial attacks have on the demand side. We analyze how manipulated price forecasts impact the decision-making in industrial demand response. To this end, we design adversarial attacks that aim to deteriorate the output of electricity price forecasting models and solve scheduling optimization problems of energy-intensive production processes using the distorted price forecasts. We make use of a generalized process model to investigate the vulnerability to adversarial attacks for a range of production scheduling problems with different levels of process flexibility. We find that adversarial attacks can erode the profits gained from demand response. However, when perturbations are limited in extent (so that they are hard to detect by the human user), demand response preserves about 90\% of its financial advantage compared to steady-state process operation. Further, we find that the impact of adversarial attacks on demand response does not only depend on the magnitude of the perturbations but rather on the orientation of the adversarial perturbations. Therefore, we argue that attack analyses should explicitly incorporate the sensitivities of scheduling optimization models into the attack design to enable more rigorous assessments of decision-making under adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [47] [When Do Geometric Algebra Layers Beat Scalarization? A Controlled Study on SO(3)-Equivariant Vector Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06634)
*Fabien Polly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了Clifford代数Cl(3,0)网络与标量化基线（点积+MLP输出等变基系数）在3D向量学习中的表现。在单阶段任务中，两者性能相当；在深度组合群操作的任务中，Cl(3,0)网络在小样本场景下显著优于标量化基线（100样本 vs 3000样本达到相同效果），且差距随旋转链长度增加而扩大。但所有模型均无法外推不变量的幅度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Clifford代数（几何代数）结构本身是否提供超越精确等变性的额外优势，即除了保证SO(3)等变性外，其丰富的代数结构是否对低样本学习有实质性贡献。

Method: 设计最小标量化基线：将不变的点积特征输入小型MLP，输出等变基{v_i, v_i × v_j}上的系数，实现精确等变。在单阶段目标（轴角旋转、叉积、中心力）、组合目标（多层旋转嵌套、通过方位映射局部力再计算力矩）以及无旋转嵌套叉积上进行对比。进行了深度消融实验、基线增强（添加三重积不变量、扩大17倍参数、对比Vector Neurons和e3nn基线、乘法系数网络）和外推实验。

Result: 1) 单阶段任务中标量化基线匹配或超越Cl(3,0)网络，训练成本更低。2) 组合群操作任务中，Cl(3,0)网络在小样本区表现优异（100样本达基线3000样本效果），差距在更长的旋转链（4次旋转）上进一步拉大。3) 无旋转的嵌套叉积任务中，标量化以24倍优势获胜。4) 所有模型在幅度外推（半径、分离位移）上均劣于常值预测器。

Conclusion: 几何代数层并非低样本3D学习的通用捷径，其优势仅体现在目标需要深度组合群元素（即多次群操作嵌套）的场景中。对于可简化为多项式不变量系数的任务，标量化方法更优。等变性本身不足以保证幅度外推能力。

Abstract: Compact networks built from Clifford algebra Cl(3,0) primitives are exactly SO(3)-equivariant and learn synthetic 3D vector laws from few samples. We ask whether the geometric algebra structure itself contributes anything beyond exact equivariance. We compare against a minimal scalarization baseline: invariant dot products fed to a small MLP that outputs coefficients on the equivariant basis {v_i, v_i x v_j}, which is also exactly equivariant. On single-stage laws (rotation by axis-angle, cross product, central force), scalarization matches or beats the Cl(3,0) network at a fraction of the training cost, so the geometric algebra adds nothing there. On compositional targets whose computation graph nests group operations (apply R2 R1 to a point; map a local force through an orientation, then take a torque), the Cl(3,0) network beats scalarization by an order of magnitude in the low-data regime, reaching with 100 samples what the baseline needs 3000 for, and the gap survives strengthening the baseline with the triple-product invariant and 17x more parameters, external Vector Neurons and e3nn baselines, and a multiplicative coefficient network. Ablations show the required network depth tracks the rotation chain length, and scalarization falls below the constant predictor on chains of four rotations. The advantage is not composition per se: on a rotation-free nested cross product, which flattens into polynomial invariant coefficients, scalarization wins by 24x. No tested model, equivariant or not, extrapolates invariant magnitudes: on radius and separation shifts every model is worse than a constant predictor once errors are normalized. We conclude that geometric algebra layers are not a general shortcut for low-data 3D learning, but become useful precisely when the target composes group elements in depth.

</details>


### [48] [Geometric--Nongeometric Optimizer Calculus: A Modular Language for Reachable Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07206)
*Zavier Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“几何—非几何优化器微积分”模块化语言，用于审计可达到的梯度方法。核心理论结果是：在远离临界点处，全正定几何严格表达所有严格下降方向。通过定义受限方向残差，证明了对角/块几何的精确表达能力，并将方向诊断与条件数复杂度分离。最终将设计问题转化为帕累托预算优化，而非单一排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应优化器（如Adam、Muon等）混合了多种机制（度量、估计、记忆、步长控制、约束、随机性、目标修改、离散化），缺乏统一的模块化语言来审计和理解不同优化器在给定预算、状态和规则约束下所能达到的方向表达能力。

Method: 构建几何模块（正定余度量族，将余切向量映射到参数空间方向）与非几何模块（信息、记忆、控制、算子、噪声、目标、离散化）的模块化语言；提出方向表达性定理；定义受限方向残差；将点态残差提升为轨迹级残差复杂度。

Result: 全正定几何在远离临界点处精确表达所有严格下降方向；给出了对角/块几何的精确表达性条件；方向级诊断与条件数复杂度可分离；高信息全度量探针在确定性二次基准上达到数值精度；Muon风格的矩阵算子更新可被该微积分审计。

Conclusion: 优化器设计应视为模块预算的帕累托优化问题，而非寻找单一通用最优排序。该工作主要贡献是理论框架和基准语言，不宣称大规模SOTA性能。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers mix several mechanisms: a metric or preconditioner maps gradients to descent directions, while estimation, memory, step-size control, constraints, stochasticity, target modification, and discretization determine which directions are available and how they are used. We introduce geometric--nongeometric optimizer calculus, a modular language for auditing reachable gradient methods under explicit oracle, budget, state, and rule constraints. The geometric module is a positive cometric family that maps covectors to parameter-space directions; the nongeometric modules are information, memory, control, operator, noise, target, and discretization mechanisms. The main formal result is a direction-expressivity theorem: away from critical points, full positive-definite geometry expresses exactly the strict descent directions. We then define restricted direction residuals for admissible metric families, prove exact expressivity conditions for diagonal and block geometries, and separate this direction-level diagnostic from condition-number geometric complexity. The resulting design problem is a Pareto optimization over module budgets, not a single universal optimizer ordering. We also lift pointwise residuals to a trajectory-level residual complexity that couples direction mismatch with the variation of the explaining geometry. We include diagnostic prototypes only as evidence for the language: a high-information full-metric probe solves deterministic quadratic benchmarks to numerical precision, while a practical Muon-style PyTorch candidate gives small-scale evidence that matrix-operator updates can be audited by the calculus. The paper is a theory and benchmark-language manuscript; it does not claim large-scale optimizer state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [49] [Optimized Instance Alteration for Explaining and Assessing Robustness of Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06637)
*Evgenii Kuriabov,David Miller,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一框架，通过可解释性优化（XA-L₀惩罚）同时诊断黑盒分类器的误分类原因并评估其鲁棒性，并引入容忍区域混淆矩阵（TOR-Confusion Matrix）量化类别间转换概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将误分类诊断和鲁棒性评估视为独立任务，缺乏统一框架；且多数方法在生成对抗性样本时未考虑修改的可解释性，导致难以理解分类器失败的根本原因。

Method: 提出一个优化框架，目标函数包含两项：①XA-L₀惩罚项，促进稀疏且可解释的修改；②分类器损失项，驱使扰动样本朝目标标签变化。基于该框架实现两个功能：通过最小化修改识别误分类原因；通过容忍区域内的扰动边界评估鲁棒性。引入TOR-Confusion Matrix，通过容忍有界扰动下的类别转移概率矩阵量化分类器脆弱性。

Result: 在图像和表格数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够同时提供可解释的误分类诊断和鲁棒性量化评估，验证了框架的统一性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了解释性与鲁棒性评估可以统一在同一优化框架下，为黑盒分类器的诊断与安全性分析提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a unified approach for diagnosing misclassification and assessing the robustness of black-box classifiers. Central to our method is an optimization framework that modifies an instance so that the classifier predicts a specified target label, while ensuring that the modification remains easily explainable. The objective function contains two components: an explainability-aware $L_0$ (XA-$L_0$) penalty that promotes sparse and interpretable modifications, and a classifier loss objective that steers the perturbed instance toward the desired output. This integrated optimization formulation is used both to identify the underlying causes of misclassification and to evaluate robustness by determining how an instance can change within a tolerance region before being reassigned to another class. To quantify robustness, we introduce the Tolerance Region Confusion Matrix (TOR-Confusion Matrix), which measures a classifier's susceptibility by modeling the class-to-class transition probabilities induced by tolerance-bounded perturbations. We validate the proposed method on both image and tabular datasets, demonstrating its ability to jointly deliver interpretability and robustness assessment.

</details>


### [50] [Latency-Aware Bid Acceptance under Operational Feasibility: A Public Benchmark with Hindsight Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07343)
*Aswin Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FreightBidBench，一个基于公开数据、可复现的在线整车货运报价接受基准测试平台，并开发了更紧的 hindsight 上界（Lagrangian-per-truck 信息松弛）以及参数化 surrogate-rollout 级联策略，在紧容量和稀缺容量场景下分别恢复 rollout 利润的约 98%，且延迟仅为 rollout 的 40-56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线货运报价接受问题的公开基准稀缺：路由基准是静态的，动态车队研究依赖私有运营商数据，导致结果难以复现和公平比较。

Method: 构建基于公共 FAF 和 USDA 卡车运费数据的闭环基准；开发两种全视界 hindsight 上界（LP 松弛和更紧的 Lagrangian-per-truck 信息松弛）；提出参数化 surrogate-rollout 级联策略，包含边界带和稀缺压力升级触发机制。

Result: Lagrangian-per-truck 松弛比 LP 松弛在紧容量场景下紧 20.7%，稀缺场景下紧 39.3%。简单策略保留 rollout 利润的 91.0%/86.5%；标准 surrogate 保留 94.2%/89.3%；级联策略在两种场景下恢复约 98% 的利润，延迟降至 rollout 的 40-56%，在紧场景下与 rollout 教师策略统计无显著差异。

Conclusion: FreightBidBench 提供了首个公开可复现的在线货运报价接受基准，所提出的级联策略能在几乎不损失利润的情况下大幅降低计算延迟。

Abstract: Online truckload bid acceptance is a closed-loop stochastic decision problem in which a carrier or broker must, in real time, accept or reject a tendered load subject to operational feasibility, fleet repositioning costs, and opportunity cost against future demand. Public, reproducible benchmarks for this problem are scarce: existing routing benchmarks are static, while dynamic-fleet studies typically rely on private operator data. We introduce FreightBidBench, a public-calibrated, dependency-free, closed-loop benchmark in which feasibility (pickup reach, appointment windows, simplified hours-of-service, stochastic yard delays) and economics (service-failure penalty, terminal fleet value, daily price-premium window) are explicit, versioned, and reproducible from public Freight Analysis Framework and U.S. Department of Agriculture truck rate data. We develop two full-horizon hindsight ceilings: a simple LP style relaxation and a tighter Lagrangian-per-truck information relaxation that retains per-truck hours-of-service and sequencing structure and is 20.7% tighter than the LP relaxation on a tight-capacity scenario and 39.3% tighter on a scarce-capacity scenario. We introduce a parametric surrogate-rollout cascade with boundary-band and scarcity-pressure escalation triggers. On ten-seed tight and scarce scenarios, the best simple policy retains 91.0% and 86.5% of rollout profit and the standard-library surrogate 94.2% and 89.3%; a cascade at a single escalation band recovers roughly 98% on both at 40-56% of rollout's mean decision latency, and on the tight scenario is statistically indistinguishable from the rollout teacher (paired-bootstrap 95% CI on the profit delta spans zero).

</details>


### [51] [UASPL: Uncertainty-Aware Self-Paced Learning with Evidential Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06638)
*Yifan Zhang,Yuxin Hu,Zhuobin Hao,Xiaozhuan Gao,Lipeng Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据神经网络的不确定性感知自步学习（UASPL），通过主观逻辑框架将预测可靠性整合到样本选择中，在多个数据集上展现出优于其他SPL方法的分类性能、可解释性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自步学习（SPL）基于损失函数值从易到难选择样本，但损失值小的样本预测结果未必可靠，即这类样本并不总是模型的简单样本。为解决这一问题，本文提出将预测可靠性纳入样本选择过程。

Method: 基于证据神经网络，在主观逻辑（Subjective Logic）框架下设计了一个通用的损失函数，该函数融合了不确定性估计，可扩展到不同的SPL变体，同时耦合了样本选择偏好，保证了样本选择过程的可解释性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，UASPL在分类性能、可解释性和泛化性方面均优于其他SPL方法。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的自步学习能有效解决传统SPL中低损失样本不可靠的问题，提升了样本选择的合理性与模型性能。

Abstract: Self-paced learning (SPL) is an effective learning paradigm that simulates the human learning process by progressing from easy to difficult samples based on the value of the loss function during the learning process. It has shown great potential in improving model performance and training efficiency. However, the prediction results of samples with smaller loss values are not necessarily reliable, indicating that such samples are not always simple samples for the model. Hence, this article proposes an uncertainty-aware self-paced learning based on evidential neural networks, termed UASPL, which integrates predictive reliability into sample selection through a general loss function within the Subjective Logic framework. This loss function incorporates uncertainty estimation and can be extended to different variants of SPL. Moreover, this loss function couples a sample selection preference, thereby ensuring the interpretability of the sample selection process. Finally, the experimental results on multiple datasets show that UASPL outperforms other SPL methods in terms of classification performance, interpretability, and generality. The source code is available at: https://github.com/treelife979/UASPL.

</details>


### [52] [The Rank-One Corner: How Much Value Equivalence Does a Task Need from a World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06640)
*Donna Vakalis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，世界模型所学到的潜在表征能捕捉多少任务所需的结构（称为"closure"），取决于训练所用的目标函数的维度，而非模型容量或观察数据。标量价值信号只安装一维投影，而高维目标能安装更多预测性维度。价值等价（Value Equivalence）不是全有或全无，而是维度性的——单奖励目标是其秩一特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估世界模型的标准通常关注重构保真度或奖励预测精度，忽略了实际任务只依赖模型中的一小部分预测坐标（即"closure"）。作者希望揭示是什么决定了模型潜在表征能捕捉到多少任务所需的结构。

Method: 在DreamerV3框架中，通过控制环境（已知ground-truth closure），系统性地改变训练目标函数的维度（从一维标量到四维向量），测量线性探针恢复的结构量（R²值），并进行容量匹配比较和原位压力检查来排除替代解释。

Result: 标量价值信号只安装一维投影（R²从0.10提升至0.76）；目标维度从1扫到4时，辅助头恰好安装对应数量的预测方向；模型自身的价值头出现相同阶梯模式（幅度衰减但秩相同）。在闭环任务中，重构信号已能安装所需结构，标量目标便足够。

Conclusion: 价值等价不是全有或全无，而是维度性的——模型安装多少任务结构取决于它被要求预测的目标的维度。单奖励目标是秩一特例。当更廉价的训练信号（如重构）已经能恢复结构时，标量目标就足够；而当需要模型捕捉更复杂的结构时，必须使用更高维的目标。

Abstract: A learned world model is usually judged by how faithfully it reconstructs its observations or predicts reward, as though quality were something the model simply has or lacks. But what a task actually needs from a model is narrower: the few predictive coordinates its queries depend on, which we call the closure. We show that how much of that closure a latent comes to represent is set not by the model's capacity or its observations but by the dimensionality of the objective it is trained against, and we measure this directly on a DreamerV3 stack in a controlled environment with known ground-truth closure. An aligned scalar value signal -- the objective at the heart of value equivalence -- installs only a one-dimensional projection of a closure that needs several dimensions: read through a single linear probe, the recoverable structure rises from R^2=0.10 to 0.76 as the scalar is replaced by the full objective. Sweeping the objective's dimensionality from one to four installs exactly that many predictive directions through an auxiliary head, and the same staircase appears -- at attenuated magnitude but the same rank -- through the model's own value head, so the dissociation is dimensional rather than an artifact of head form. Capacity-matched comparisons and in-situ pressure checks rule out the obvious alternatives. The law governs a regime, and we measure its boundary: on a companion closed-loop task whose structure is observable frame by frame, reconstruction installs that structure and the scalar objective suffices -- the objective decides what a latent represents exactly where cheaper training signals cannot already recover it. Value equivalence is thus not all-or-nothing but dimensional: the familiar single-reward objective is its rank-one corner, and a model installs as much of a task's structure as the objective it is asked to predict.

</details>


### [53] [Diffusion enabled Optimal Transport distances for graph matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06646)
*Iman Seyedi,Francesco Archetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DsrFGW方法，通过扩散过程统一节点特征和图结构的图比较，在稀疏/噪声/局部观测图上优于传统半松弛FGW，在36个合成图匹配任务中准确率提升0-20个百分点，中等难度下ARI从负值提升至有效水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半松弛FGW（srFGW）在处理稀疏、噪声或部分观测图时性能不足，缺乏对信息传播模式的建模能力。

Method: 在FGW框架中引入扩散过程，使信息能够在节点间传播，同时捕获局部和全局结构模式，降低对噪声或缺失边的敏感性。

Result: 在36个合成图匹配任务（简单/中等/困难）上全面优于srFGW，准确率提升0-20个百分点；中等难度下srFGW的ARI为负值（差于随机），而DsrFGW取得有效提升；严重噪声下92%任务改善聚类质量。

Conclusion: DsrFGW是一种稳健的图比较框架，能有效应对结构不确定性，最优扩散尺度可自适应问题难度。

Abstract: This paper introduces Diffusion Semi-Relaxed Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (DsrFGW), a novel method for graph comparison that unifies node features and structural connectivity through optimal transport. While traditional Gromov-Wasserstein and semi-relaxed variants (srGW, srFGW) capture graph structure, they often struggle with sparse, noisy, or partially observed graphs. Inspired by Graph Diffusion Distance, which posits graphs are similar if they enable similar information transmission patterns, DsrFGW incorporates diffusion processes allowing information propagation across nodes, capturing local and global structural patterns while reducing sensitivity to noise or missing edges. An extensive evaluation on 36 synthetic pairwise graph matching tasks (easy, medium, hard) demonstrates consistent superiority over srFGW, achieving accuracy improvements of 0-20 percentage points and dramatic Adjusted Rand Index (ARI) gains: in medium-difficulty scenarios, srFGW often achieves negative ARI (worse than random) while DsrFGW offers better performance in terms of both internal and external clustering quality measures (i.e., Adjusted Rank Index and Accuracy with respect to the true underlying clusters, respectively). Even under severe noise, DsrFGW improves clustering quality in 92% of the synthetic tasks with optimal diffusion scales adapting to problem difficulty, establishing DsrFGW as a robust framework for graph comparison under structural uncertainty.

</details>


### [54] [Final Checkpoints Are Not Enough: Analyzing Latent Reasoning Faithfulness Along Training Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06648)
*Hengyu Jin,Shu Yang,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了隐式推理方法在训练过程中忠实度的演化，发现输出层面的不忠实行为呈现不同轨迹，隐式推理步骤的因果贡献随训练衰减，且该轨迹因答案格式而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅在收敛检查点发现隐式推理不忠实行为（如推理步骤可被替换而不改变答案），但未探究这些行为在训练过程中的形成机制。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对输入施加可验证的反事实编辑（输出层面），以及对隐式推理步骤施加噪声消融激活补丁（激活层面），追踪不同隐式推理范式在保存检查点上的忠实度演化。

Result: (i) 输出层面：不同隐式推理方法在收敛时看似相似不忠实，但遵循不同轨迹；(ii) 激活层面：隐式推理步骤对最终答案的因果贡献随训练衰减，输出层面翻转的例子恰好是贡献衰减的例子；(iii) 激活层面轨迹因答案格式而异：二选一时衰减，开放式解码时上升。

Conclusion: 隐式推理的忠实度依赖于训练阶段和答案格式，这为该领域的可解释性和可靠性研究提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Latent reasoning methods perform multi-step inference entirely in the model's continuous hidden states, promising more compact and efficient reasoning. However, these opaque hidden states raise a question of faithfulness: whether these latent reasoning steps causally drive the final answer. Prior work investigates this question at converged checkpoints and reports several unfaithful behaviors, such as latent reasoning steps that can be replaced without changing the answer, but leaves how these behaviors form during training unexamined. We instead track how faithfulness evolves across saved checkpoints for different latent reasoning paradigms, applying a verifiable counterfactual edit on the input and a noise-ablation activation patch on the latent reasoning steps. We find that (i) at the output level, latent reasoning methods can look similarly unfaithful at convergence under counterfactual edits while following qualitatively divergent trajectories; (ii) at the activation level, the causal contribution of latent reasoning steps to the final answer decays across training for both paradigms, with the examples that flip on the output side in (i) also being the examples on which this contribution decays; and (iii) the activation-level trajectory diverges by answer format, decaying on binary choice and rising on open-ended decoding. These findings highlight that latent reasoning faithfulness depends on training stage and answer format.

</details>


### [55] [From Jumps to Signatures: a Generative Method for Temporal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06652)
*Niels Cariou-Kotlarek,Vasileios Lampos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出sigTPP，一种基于粗糙路径签名的时间点过程生成模型，通过将跳跃路径连续提升（interarrival embedding），实现了对事件序列的路径级建模、训练和评估，并在多个指标上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有签名方法不适用于cadlag路径（TPP的跳跃路径），神经TPP模型缺乏全局序列级损失，且变长事件序列的评估缺乏分布差异度量。

Method: 提出interarrival embedding，一种从跳跃路径到有界变差连续路径的稳定单射提升，从而将签名方法扩展到离散事件序列；基于此构建sigTPP生成模型，使用路径级损失训练；推导三种分布差异度量用于评估。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，sigTPP在8个互补指标上获得最佳平均排名，在64%的指标-数据集对中达到最优或与最强基线在标准误差内，相对得分上平均至少提升19%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种将签名方法扩展到事件序列的统一路径框架，包括理论上的嵌入方法、实践上的生成模型以及评估上的分布差异度量，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Rough path signatures are a universal feature map for continuous paths and, via the expected signature, characterise path distributions. These guarantees do not directly extend to cadlag paths of Temporal Point Processes (TPPs), limiting the use of signature methods for event sequences. Furthermore, neural TPP models, including recent generative approaches, optimise per-event objectives with no global sequence-level loss, while evaluation of variable-length event sequences lacks distributional discrepancy measures. This paper proposes a common pathwise framework for addressing these limitations. We introduce the interarrival embedding, a stable, injective lift from jump paths to continuous paths of bounded variation, extending signature methods to discrete event sequences. Our theoretical contributions give rise to sigTPP, the first signature-based generative model for TPPs, trained using a path-level loss on complete trajectories. We further analyse the space of counting paths and derive three distributional discrepancies, providing mathematically justified tools for evaluating generative TPP models. Across synthetic and real-world datasets, sigTPP achieves the best average rank based on eight complementary metrics, outperforms or is within a standard error of the strongest baseline in 64% of the dataset-metric pairs, and according to a relative score, improves against every baseline by at least 19% on average.

</details>


### [56] [Trees from Marginals: Autoregressive drafting with factorized priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06763)
*Yuma Oda,Ryan Mathieu,Roman Knyazhitskiy,Artur Chakhvadze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Weaver是一种轻量级自回归适配器，通过从因子化草稿模型的top-K边缘分布构建提议树，恢复条件依赖性，结合免回滚树验证算法和优化CUDA内核，实现了4.37倍的自回归解码加速，比DFlash基线提升24.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码通过额外计算提升自回归语言模型的交互性，但因子化草稿模型因独立性假设导致接受率随预算增长急剧下降，需要解决这一局限性。

Method: 提出Weaver轻量级自回归适配器，从因子化草稿器的top-K边缘分布构建提议树，恢复条件依赖；针对Gated Delta Net层推导免回滚树验证算法，并在SGLang中实现优化的CUDA内核。

Result: 自回归解码加速4.37倍，比DFlash基线提升24.7%。

Conclusion: 通过模型和系统两方面的协同贡献，有效解决了因子化草稿模型的条件独立性问题，显著提升了投机解码效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding greatly increases the interactivity of autoregressive language models by trading off computation for extra tokens generated in a single forward pass. Factorized draft models are especially efficient because they predict future-token marginals in parallel, but their independence assumption causes acceptance rates to degrade sharply as the speculative budget grows. We analyze this limitation and introduce Weaver, a lightweight autoregressive adapter that constructs proposal trees from the top-K marginals of a factorized drafter. Weaver restores conditional dependencies between proposed tokens while avoiding a full-vocabulary projection. To support fast verification for models with Gated Delta Net layers, we derive a rollback-free tree-verification algorithm and implement optimized CUDA kernels in SGLang. By combining these model and systems contributions we achieve a 4.37-fold speedup over autoregressive decoding, and outperform a highly optimized DFlash baseline by 24.7%.

</details>


### [57] [Geometric Self-Distillation for Reasoning Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06855)
*Josip Jukić,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoSD 是一种几何自蒸馏目标函数，通过 Hellinger 损失和 Fisher-Rao 距离近端惩罚来抑制特权上下文自蒸馏中的预测漂移，在保持分布内收益的同时，在数学推理任务上将 OOD 准确率提升 5.7-8.6 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在特权上下文自蒸馏中，教师模型因看到额外信息（如提示或完整解答轨迹）而对某些续写过于自信，但学生模型缺乏这些信息。蒸馏损失在师生分歧最大处最强，导致学生预测行为逐渐漂移，损害了分布外（OOD）推理能力。

Method: GeoSD 包含两个互补机制：(1) Hellinger 损失，根据学生已与教师共享的重叠程度缩放教师偏好，减弱学生对尚无法支持的 token 的蒸馏拉力；(2) 近端惩罚项，以 Fisher-Rao 距离衡量学生预测偏离最近检查点的程度。两种距离都定义在同一 next-token 分布几何上，并使用自然梯度更新在该几何空间中更新参数。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，使用三种模型族（参数规模 1.7B 到 32B），GeoSD 在保持分布内自蒸馏收益的同时，将平均 OOD 准确率提升 5.7-8.6 个百分点。分析发现，标准匹配失败是因为通过从高熵状态中抽取备选 token 的质量来赢得与教师的一致，导致在错误答案上自信地一致，而 GeoSD 保持了这些备选 token 的可访问性。

Conclusion: GeoSD 通过几何视角的蒸馏目标函数有效解决特权上下文自蒸馏中的漂移问题，在数学推理任务上同时兼顾了分布内性能和分布外泛化能力，并通过自然梯度更新实现稳定的训练动态。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is a practical post-training recipe for large language models, supplying dense teacher supervision on the student's own trajectories. In privileged-context self-distillation, teacher and student are the same model conditioned on the same prefix, but the teacher also sees a hint or the full solution trace. This makes supervision abundant but harder to trust: the teacher can be confident about continuations its privileged view makes obvious but the student cannot yet justify. The distillation pull is strongest where teacher and student disagree most, and over many updates it accumulates into drift that degrades out-of-distribution (OOD) reasoning. We introduce GeoSD, a geometric self-distillation objective that treats this drift as movement in the student's predictive behavior and counters it in two complementary ways. A Hellinger loss scales each teacher preference by the overlap the student already shares with it, attenuating the pull on tokens the student cannot yet support. Since these pulls still compound over training, a proximal term penalizes how far the student's predictions drift from a recent checkpoint, measured as a Fisher-Rao distance. Both are distances in the same geometry of next-token distributions, and a natural-gradient update takes its steps in that geometry rather than in parameter space. Across mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three model families, GeoSD preserves the in-distribution gains of self-distillation while improving average OOD accuracy by 5.7-8.6 points over the base model, with gains holding across model scales from 1.7B to 32B. Analyzing why standard matching fails out of distribution, we find it wins agreement with the teacher by draining mass from alternatives at high-entropy states, resulting in confident agreement on wrong answers, whereas GeoSD keeps those alternatives in reach.

</details>


### [58] [Efficient Bayesian Deep Ensembles via Analytic Predictive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06776)
*Sina Aghaee Dabaghan Fard,Marie Maros,Jaesung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的贝叶斯深度集成方法用于预测回归，通过低维集成表示、闭式贝叶斯聚合和独立集成训练三个核心设计，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时提供了可解释性和校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度集成方法在不确定性校准和可解释性方面存在不足，而传统贝叶斯方法计算成本高、难以扩展。该方法旨在结合贝叶斯推断的统计严谨性与深度集成的可扩展性。

Method: 三个关键组件：(i) 低维集成表示——将预测表示为少量训练好的神经预测器的组合，计算成本取决于集成规模而非数据集规模；(ii) 闭式贝叶斯聚合——使用贝叶斯线性回归组合集成预测，得到可解释的后验权重和校准的不确定性，无需近似推断；(iii) 独立集成训练——分别训练多个神经网络，产生多样化的预测表示以提高鲁棒性和不确定性校准。

Result: 在标准回归基准测试上，该方法在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，跨多种设置提供了可靠的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该方法兼顾了预测性能、可解释性、不确定性校准和计算效率，可作为更广泛学习系统中的组件使用。

Abstract: We introduce an efficient Bayesian deep ensemble method for predictive regression designed to enhance interpretability while maintaining competitive predictive performance and computational efficiency. Our method combines the statistical rigor of Bayesian inference with the scalability of deep ensembles, providing calibrated uncertainty estimates that enable its use not only for standalone prediction but also as a component within broader learning systems. To achieve these goals, our work relies on three key design components: (i) low-dimensional ensemble representation: predictions are expressed as a combination of a small number of trained neural predictors, enabling scalable inference whose cost depends on ensemble size rather than dataset size; (ii) closed-form Bayesian aggregation: ensemble predictions are combined using Bayesian linear regression, yielding interpretable posterior weights and calibrated uncertainty without approximate inference; and (iii) Independent ensemble training: multiple neural networks are trained separately, producing diverse predictive representations that improve robustness and uncertainty calibration. Empirical results on standard regression benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves competitive predictive performance while maintaining reliable uncertainty estimates across settings.

</details>


### [59] [Dissociating the Internal Representations of Sycophancy in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07003)
*Anthony Baez,Sheer Karny,Pat Pataranutaporn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将LLM的谄媚行为分解为事实型和观点型两种子类型，通过探针和引导向量实验发现不同模型对这两种子类型的表征方式存在差异（统一或分离），为理解复杂模型行为的表征结构提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谄媚行为（sycophancy）在LLM中表现多样且情境各异，但现有研究通常将其视为单一行为，缺乏对其内部机制是否反映这种多面性的深入探究。

Method: 将谄媚行为解耦为事实型（基于可验证主张）和观点型（基于主观信念）两种子类型，分别训练线性探针和构建引导向量，评估一种子类型上的表征能否迁移到另一子类型，以此衡量它们共享表征的程度。

Result: 不同LLM对这两种子类型的表征方式不同，有的模型表现出更统一的表征，有的则表现出更分离且存在因果干扰的表征。

Conclusion: 该解耦方法为研究复杂模型行为的表征结构提供了有前景的分析框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently exhibit sycophancy, where they agree with a user's statement even when incorrect. While sycophancy is often treated as a single defined behavior, it can manifest in substantially distinct ways and circumstances, raising the question of whether this multi-faceted nature is reflected in its internal mechanisms. To address this gap, we dissociate the representations of sycophancy into factual and opinion subtypes -- motivated by the distinction between verifiable claims and subjective beliefs. We train linear probes and construct steering vectors on activations of one subtype and evaluate their transfer to the other subtype to measure to what extent they share representations. We find evidence that different LLMs represent these subtypes differently, with either more unified or more distinct and causally interfering representations. This method of dissociation offers a promising framework for studying the representational structure of complex model behaviors.

</details>


### [60] [On Explicit Super-Expressive Approximation for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06781)
*Feng-Lei Fan,Ze-Yu Li,Chen-Yu Wang,Jian-Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入中国剩余定理作为构造性编码机制，解决了固定架构神经网络在显式参数界下逼近 Lipschitz 连续函数和 Hölder 光滑函数时的参数-误差量化权衡问题，得到了参数大小与逼近误差之间的非渐近显式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超表达能力固定大小网络的研究缺乏参数大小与逼近误差之间定量、非渐近的表征，即缺少显式的参数-误差权衡关系。

Method: 引入中国剩余定理作为构造性编码机制，构建固定宽深度的网络：对 Lipschitz 连续函数使用宽度 $\max\{D,4\}$、深度 5 的网络；对 Hölder 光滑函数使用宽度 $\max\{2D, D+5N+1\}$、深度 $r+9$ 的网络。

Result: 对 Lipschitz 连续函数，得到显式参数-误差权衡；对 Hölder 光滑函数，参数大小满足 $\log_2 \mathcal{P} = \mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2D/(r+\gamma)}\log(1/\varepsilon))$，这是参数有界-架构无界范式下的对偶结果。

Conclusion: 首次在固定架构神经网络中建立了显式的参数界与逼近误差之间的非渐近量化关系，填补了超表达能力网络的理论空白。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the fixed-architecture neural network approximation with explicit parameter bounds and elementary activations. While prior work demonstrated super-expressive approximation using fixed-size networks, they lack quantitative and non-asymptotic characterizations of parameter magnitude with respect to the approximation error. We resolve this issue by introducing the Chinese Remainder Theorem as a constructive encoding mechanism. For Lipschitz continuous functions on $[0,1]^D$, we construct a width-$\max\{D,4\}$, depth-$5$ network with explicit parameter-error trade-offs. For Hölder-smooth functions in $C^{r,γ}_A\left([0,1]^D\right)$, our fixed network of width $\max\{2D,\ D+5N+1\}$ and depth $r + 9$ achieves the parameter magnitude $\mathcal{P}$ bounded by $\log_2 \mathcal{P}=\mathcal{O}\bigl(\varepsilon^{-2D/(r+γ)}\log(1/\varepsilon)\bigr)$. This is the dual result compared to those in the parameter-bounded and architecture-unbounded paradigm.

</details>


### [61] [Enhancing deep learning models for time series classification via knowledge distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06796)
*Javidan Abdullayev,Maxime Devanne,Jonathan Weber,Germain Forestier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了知识蒸馏（KD）在时间序列分类（TSC）中的有效性，涵盖FCN、Inception和ConvTran三种架构，在UCR基准数据集上的实验表明KD对中等复杂度的学生模型效果最佳，其中蒸馏后的FCN参数量减少38倍、Inception学生模型以42%更少参数保持相近性能、ConvTran学生模型在2个注意力头时提升最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在时间序列分析等领域取得显著成功，但高计算和内存需求限制了其在资源受限环境中的部署。知识蒸馏（KD）有望通过将知识从大型教师模型迁移到小型学生模型来解决这一挑战，但KD在时间序列分类（TSC）中的有效性尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 选取三种代表性架构（经典全卷积网络FCN、卷积Inception模型和基于Transformer的ConvTran模型），通过修改卷积滤波器数量、Inception模块和注意力头等架构组件，在UCR Archive（最大的时间序列基准数据集）上系统评估KD对学生模型性能的影响。

Result: 1) KD对中等复杂度的学生模型效果最佳，该结论在三种架构上一致成立；2) 蒸馏后的FCN学生模型参数量减少38倍；3) 蒸馏后的Inception学生模型以42%更少参数达到与教师相近的性能；4) 蒸馏后的ConvTran学生模型在2个注意力头时提升最显著。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏在时间序列分类中是一种有效的模型压缩方法，能显著降低模型参数量同时维持性能，且对中等复杂度的学生模型收益最大。研究者已开源代码以促进可重复性和后续研究。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved remarkable success in various domains including time series analysis, computer vision and natural language processing. However, high computational and memory demands of state-of-the-art architectures pose challenges for deployment in resource-limited environments. Knowledge Distillation (KD) addresses this by transferring knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller, more efficient student model while maintaining competitive performance. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of KD for Time Series Classification (TSC) across three architectures: the classical Fully Convolutional Network (FCN), the convolutional Inception model and the transformer-based ConvTran model. We evaluate our approach on UCR Archive, the largest benchmark repository of time series datasets, by modifying architectural components such as convolutional filters, Inception modules and attention heads across the three architectures. Our results consistently show that KD most effectively benefits student models of intermediate complexity across all three architectures, with the distilled FCN student reducing parameters by a factor of 38, the distilled Inception student achieving nearly the same performance as the teacher with 42% fewer parameters and the distilled ConvTran student with 2 attention heads showing the most significant improvement through distillation. To encourage further research and reproducibility, we provide our implementation at https://github.com/MSD-IRIMAS/KD-4-TSC.

</details>


### [62] [What Predicts Correctness in Text-to-SQL? A Selective-Prediction Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06799)
*Robert Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 评估AI生成SQL查询的不确定性，发现基于验证的信号（LLM评判器）优于黑箱和白箱信号（AUROC最高0.82），而微调验证器虽在分布内表现良好（0.77-0.79）但跨模式泛化能力差（降至0.66），最终结论是：SQL正确性不确定性依赖于推理信号，跨模式泛化需要大规模冻结推理模型而非微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI生成SQL查询的不确定性评估，即判断查询是否正确的信号预测能力，以区分正确与错误查询。

Method: 在BIRD和Spider基准上，使用AUROC评估多种信号：黑箱信号（字符串、结构、执行自一致性、模式相关性、可执行性）、白箱log-probability、基于LLM评判器的验证信号（单模型及多提供商集成），以及微调验证器（编码器和生成式）在分布内和跨模式场景下的表现。

Result: 黑箱和白箱信号AUROC约0.61-0.68（字符串自一致性最强0.675）；LLM评判器达0.72-0.78，双提供商集成达0.82（ECE 0.03）；微调验证器分布内0.77-0.79，跨模式降至约0.66，扩大模型规模、增加模式多样性、蒸馏推理链等均无法弥合差距。

Conclusion: SQL查询正确性不确定性评估中，推理型信号（LLM评判器）效果最优；微调验证器仅在分布内有效，跨模式泛化需要大规模冻结推理模型。

Abstract: Evaluating uncertainty in AI-generated SQL queries requires estimating whether a query is correct, where correct means it executes to the same result as a human-written reference. We study which signals predict correctness on hard multi-table text-to-SQL, using AUROC to measure how well each ranks correct queries above incorrect ones. On BIRD and Spider, black-box signals such as string, structural, and execution self-consistency, a schema-relevance score, and query executability all fall between about 0.61 and 0.68 AUROC, with string self-consistency strongest at 0.675; white-box log-probability is similar (0.67). The signals that move past this ceiling are verification-based: an LLM judge scores from 0.72 (GPT-4o-mini) to 0.78 (Claude). Judges from different providers make different errors, so a two-provider ensemble reaches 0.82 AUROC with a well-calibrated probability (expected calibration error 0.03) and supports useful abstention frontiers (for example, answering 27% of questions at 24% selective risk) where self-consistency offers no valid low-risk subset. The pattern holds across two benchmarks, two generators, and two judge providers. We also ask whether a verifier can be trained. Fine-tuned verifiers, both encoder and generative, reach about 0.77 to 0.79 AUROC in-distribution but fall to about 0.66 on unseen schemas; scaling to 7B, adding schema diversity, distilling a strong judge's rationales, and cross-benchmark training all fail to close that gap. Cross-schema transfer appears to track model scale and reasoning rather than fine-tuning. In practice, correctness uncertainty for text-to-SQL lives in reasoning-based signals: a fine-tuned verifier is a good in-domain tool, but a verifier that generalizes across schemas currently means a large frozen reasoning model.

</details>


### [63] [FourierQK: Spectral Preprocessing of Query-Key Projections Improves Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07478)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对注意力机制中的Query和Key投影进行FFT频谱预处理，在字符级语言建模中显著优于标准点积注意力。四个学习到的频率（对应段落、子段落、短语、词尺度）将困惑度降低79%（val=0.309，Delta=+1.166），而随机投影无提升。该方法的非因果性（双边FFT核）定义了与因果频谱注意力的架构边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在注意力机制的Q/K投影中引入频谱预处理，以改善标准点积注意力在字符级语言建模上的性能瓶颈，验证全局频域混合是否优于局部度量扭曲。

Method: 对Query和Key投影进行FFT频谱预处理（固定随机滤波、单个学习频率、多个学习频率），保持完整注意力分数结构，与FNet（替换注意力为傅里叶混合）不同。对比随机正交/非正交投影，以及因果滤波器（高斯、墨西哥帽、Morlet）的基线。

Result: 四个学习频率收敛到近几何多尺度排序（49, 27, 10, 6 tokens/周期），对应段落到词尺度。单频率验证：均值val=0.236，std=0.019。随机投影无提升。因果滤波器无改善。所有结果通过shuffled-validation诊断验证无位置泄露。
